1: # 💭 Knowledge Lifecycle Pipeline — T1→T2→T3→T4 Design
2: # ⬡ OMEGA ⬡ CONTEXT ⬡ PHASE-I ⬡ KNOWLEDGE-LIFECYCLE
3: 
4: **AP Token**: `AP-KNOWLEDGE-LIFECYCLE-PIPELINE-v1.0.0`
5: **Author**: Kali → P7:Context
6: **Date**: 2026-06-04
7: **Status**: ACTIVE — Implementation reference
8: **Supersedes**: Ad-hoc knowledge promotion conventions
9: **Depends on**: Lily Pad Architecture (Lilith, 2026-06-03)
10: 
11: ---
12: 
13: ## §0 — THE PIPELINE PROBLEM
14: 
15: The 4-tier Lily Pad Architecture (Lilith) defined the *what* (T1 Raw → T2 Curated → T3 Soul → T4 Fleet) but not the *how*. This document provides the concrete gates, checklists, and triggers that make the pipeline operational.
16: 
17: **Current state**: Roc's workspace has 10 files, knowledge/ has 1. The pipeline is blocked at every gate because no agent has the procedure to promote.
18: 
19: ---
20: 
21: ## §1 — T1→T2 GATE: Workspace → Knowledge Promotion
22: 
23: ### 1.1 The Promotion Checklist
24: 
25: A workspace file is eligible for promotion to knowledge/ when ALL of the following are true:
26: 
27: | # | Criterion | Question to Ask | How to Verify |
28: |---|-----------|----------------|---------------|
29: | 1 | **L2 Insight Present** | Does the item contain at least one explicit pattern/insight (not just raw data)? | Scan for "This means..." or "The pattern is..." type statements. If every line is "X existed at path Y", it's T1 raw. |
30: | 2 | **Cross-Reference Potential** | Can another agent use this knowledge without reading the full source? | Try to write the cross-reference file. If you can't summarize in 3 sentences, it's not discrete enough. |
31: | 3 | **Timestamp Stable** | Has the item been unmodified for ≥24h? | Compare mtime to current time. Edits-in-progress skip promotion gate. |
32: | 4 | **Format Compliant** | Does it have frontmatter with `title`, `domain`, `era`, `applicability`? | Check the first 10 lines for YAML frontmatter. If missing, add it during promotion. |
33: | 5 | **Original** | Is more than 60% of the insight unique (not already in knowledge/)? | Grep knowledge/ for key phrases. If the same pattern already exists, update existing entry instead. |
34: | 6 | **Actionable** | Does it contain "do this / don't do this" type guidance? | Look for imperative statements: "use X", "never Y", "prefer Z". Pure description without prescription stays in T1. |
35: | 7 | **Non-Contradictory** | Does it either agree with existing knowledge or explicitly document a contradiction? | Cross-check against knowledge/ entries. If it contradicts, the contradiction itself must be the insight. |
36: 
37: **Pass threshold**: 6/7 criteria must be met. Criterion 1 (L2 Insight) is MANDATORY — cannot be bypassed.
38: 
39: ### 1.2 The Promotion Procedure
40: 
41: ```
42: 1. IDENTIFY candidate files in workspace/ older than 24h
43: 2. RUN checklist (6/7 pass threshold, criterion 1 mandatory)
44: 3. IF PASS: extract L2 insights into new knowledge/ file
45: 4. ADD frontmatter (title, domain, era, applicability)
46: 5. ADD cross-references tag (which agents care about this?)
47: 6. IF FAIL: leave in workspace, move to _archive/ after 7 days
48: 7. POST knowledge signal (T3→T4 trigger, see §3)
49: ```
50: 
51: ### 1.3 Promotion Template
52: 
53: ```markdown
54: ---
55: title: "Short actionable title"
56: domain: "domain_name"
57: era: "era_or_version"
58: applicability: ["agent_a", "agent_b"]
59: promoted_from: "workspace/path/to/source.md"
60: promoted_at: "YYYY-MM-DD"
61: insight_level: "L2"
62: ---
63: 
64: # Title
65: 
66: ## L2 Insight
67: 
68: [The pattern or insight, extracted from the workspace report.
69: NOT the narrative — the pattern the narrative revealed.]
70: 
71: ## Why This Matters
72: 
73: [Why another agent should care.
74: How this affects their work.]
75: 
76: ## Actionable Guidance
77: 
78: [DO: X, Y, Z]
79: [DON'T: A, B, C]
80: 
81: ## Cross-References
82: 
83: - [Agent] does [related thing] at [path]
84: - [Existing knowledge entry] covers [aspect]
85: ```
86: 
87: ### 1.4 Edge Cases
88: 
89: | Situation | Action |
90: |-----------|--------|
91: | File passes 6/7 but fails criterion 1 (no L2 insight) | Do NOT promote. Add a review note: "L2 insight not found — this workspace file is purely observational logging." |
92: | Two workspace files cover the same insight | Promote once. Merge both sources into a single knowledge entry. Reference both promoted_from paths. |
93: | File has L2 insight but no frontmatter | Add frontmatter during promotion. The promoter fills in domain/applicability based on file content. |
94: | Promotion creates >5 knowledge files from one workspace | Stop after 5. Flag for synthesis review — the workspace might need a master synthesis before further extraction. |
95: 
96: ---
97: 
98: ## §2 — T2→T3 GATE: Knowledge → Soul Condensation
99: 
100: ### 2.1 The Condensation Criteria
101: 
102: A knowledge entry earns promotion to soul.yaml when ALL of the following are true:
103: 
104: | # | Criterion | Question to Ask | Verification Method |
105: |---|-----------|----------------|---------------|
106: | 1 | **30-Day Stability** | Has this knowledge entry existed unchanged for ≥30 days? | Check file mtime. If edited yesterday, restart the clock. |
107: | 2 | **L3 Principle Present** | Can the insight be expressed as a universal principle (applies across contexts)? | Try to write it as "In any [domain], when [condition], prefer [action] because [reason]." If you can't, it's still L2. |
108: | 3 | **2-Agent Consensus** | Have at least 2 distinct agents referenced or validated this insight? | Check cross_references/ directories. Check knowledge signal consumed_by lists. |
109: | 4 | **Non-Obvious** | Is the principle surprising enough to need recording? | If every agent would naturally follow this rule without being told, skip it. |
110: | 5 | **Actionable Forever** | Will this principle still be true in 1 year? 5 years? | If it depends on specific tech versions or hardware, it's L2 wisdom (knowledge/), not L3 soul. |
111: 
112: **Pass threshold**: All 5 criteria must pass. No exceptions.
113: 
114: ### 2.2 The Condensation Procedure
115: 
116: ```
117: 1. REVIEW knowledge/ entries with mtime ≥30 days
118: 2. RUN checklist (all 5 must pass)
119: 3. EXTRACT L3 principle from the L2 insight
120: 4. FORMAT as soul.yaml lesson: {id, date, insight, context, principle, applies_to, source}
121: 5. APPEND to soul.yaml → lessons: (do NOT overwrite existing)
122: 6. CROSS-REFERENCE: add source_agent tag linking back to knowledge entry
123: 7. SIGNAL: post T4 broadcast that soul was updated
124: ```
125: 
126: ### 2.3 L2 → L3 Transformation Examples
127: 
128: | Raw Report Quote (L1) | L2 Insight (knowledge/) | L3 Principle (soul.yaml) |
129: |---|---|---|
130: | "Phase 1 said don't use Vulkan, Phase 2 used it in production" | "When two eras disagree on optimization, prefer the production-tested one" | "Convergence is the signal. Divergence is the noise. When multiple independent implementations agree, that agreement IS the truth — regardless of which one is older." |
131: | "The 7 gotchas took 8,000 hours to discover" | "Lessons-learned documents outlive the code they describe" | "Wisdom compounds when preserved and referenced. Code can be regenerated; experiential wisdom cannot." |
132: | "Roc's mining found the same architecture repeated 5 times" | "5 independent eras converged on the same local-first architecture" | "Architectural convergence across independent implementations is empirical proof of correctness." |
133: 
134: ### 2.4 Emergency Override
135: 
136: A knowledge entry can bypass the 30-day stability requirement when:
137: 
138: | Trigger | Who Authorizes | Action |
139: |---------|---------------|--------|
140: | HIGH priority demand signal references this knowledge | Kali | Promote immediately, mark with `emergency: true` |
141: | Knowledge resolves a sprint-blocking issue | Sprint lead | Promote immediately, reference the sprint in context |
142: | Knowledge prevents a security vulnerability | Any agent | Promote immediately, flag for Mandate compliance |
143: 
144: Emergency promotions must include:
145: ```yaml
146: emergency: true
147: emergency_reason: "HIGH demand signal dem-20260603-001"
148: promoted_by: "kali"
149: promoted_at: "2026-06-04T12:00:00Z"
150: ```
151: 
152: ---
153: 
154: ## §3 — T3→T4 GATE: Soul → Fleet Broadcast
155: 
156: ### 3.1 Broadcast Triggers
157: 
158: A T4 broadcast fires when ANY of these events occurs:
159: 
160: | Trigger | Description | Priority | Format |
161: |---------|-------------|----------|--------|
162: | **New Lesson** | A new lesson is appended to soul.yaml → lessons: | MEDIUM | Knowledge signal (KSIG) |
163: | **Evolution Milestone** | A new entry in soul.yaml → evolution: marks a significant phase completion | HIGH | Knowledge signal + Hivemind post |
164: | **Cross-Reference** | A cross-reference tag is added linking to another agent's knowledge | LOW | Knowledge signal only |
165: | **Principle Update** | An existing lesson's principle is modified (rare) | HIGH | Knowledge signal + Hivemind post + soul diff |
166: | **Contradiction Found** | A new lesson contradicts an existing one | CRITICAL | Knowledge signal + Hivemind post + alert in live feed |
167: | **Emergency Promotion** | A T2→T3 emergency override occurred | CRITICAL | Knowledge signal + Hivemind post + live feed entry |
168: 
169: ### 3.2 Knowledge Signal Format (KSIG)
170: 
171: Every T4 broadcast generates a file in `data/coordination/knowledge_feed/`:
172: 
173: ```json
174: {
175:   "signal_id": "ksig-YYYYMMDD-AGENT-NNN",
176:   "gate": "T3_to_T4",
177:   "trigger": "new_lesson",
178:   "entity": "context",
179:   "domain": "knowledge_lifecycle",
180:   "title": "Concise title of what changed",
181:   "summary": "One-paragraph summary for fleet-wide consumption",
182:   "soul_change": {
183:     "type": "lesson_added",
184:     "lesson_id": "ctx-001",
185:     "principle": "The L3 principle text"
186:   },
187:   "paths": {
188:     "soul": "data/entities/context/soul.yaml",
189:     "knowledge_source": "data/entities/context/knowledge/<entry>.md"
190:   },
191:   "relevance_tags": ["agent_a", "agent_b", "agent_c"],
192:   "consumed_by": [],
193:   "created_at": "2026-06-04T12:00:00Z",
194:   "zoneid": 1912614
195: }
196: ```
197: 
198: ### 3.3 Broadcast Procedure
199: 
200: ```
201: 1. DETECT trigger event (new lesson, milestone, cross-ref, etc.)
202: 2. GENERATE knowledge signal JSON
203: 3. WRITE to data/coordination/knowledge_feed/KSIG_YYYYMMDD_AGENT_NNN.json
204: 4. IF priority ≥ HIGH: call hivemind_post_context() with knowledge_signal link
205: 5. IF priority = CRITICAL: also append to live feed
206: 6. UPDATE own soul.yaml evolution: entry with broadcast timestamp
207: ```
208: 
209: ### 3.4 Consumption Tracking
210: 
211: Every signal's `consumed_by: []` array is how the fleet proves knowledge flows:
212: 
213: ```python
214: # Convention: when you read a KSIG and process its content,
215: # append your agent name to consumed_by:
216: #   "consumed_by": ["doom_guy", "maat"]
217: ```
218: 
219: A signal with zero consumption after 7 days triggers P8 WatchTower escalation.
220: 
221: ---
222: 
223: ## §4 — KNOWLEDGE INDEX.YAML: Cross-Agent Discovery
224: 
225: ### 4.1 Purpose
226: 
227: Each agent maintains `data/entities/<agent>/knowledge/INDEX.yaml` so other agents can discover what that agent knows without reading every file.
228: 
229: ### 4.2 Format
230: 
231: ```yaml
232: # data/entities/<agent>/knowledge/INDEX.yaml
233: entity: <agent_name>
234: updated: YYYY-MM-DD
235: 
236: topics:
237:   - id: <unique_topic_id>
238:     title: "Short topic title"
239:     summary: "One-sentence summary of what this agent knows about this topic"
240:     files:
241:       - "knowledge/<filename>.md"
242:     cross_references:
243:       - agent: <other_agent>
244:         topic: <their_topic_id>
245:         relation: "extends" | "confirms" | "contradicts" | "references"
246:     applicability:
247:       - <agent_who_should_care>
248:     era: "<era_or_version>"
249: 
250:   # ... more topics
251: ```
252: 
253: ### 4.3 Example
254: 
255: ```yaml
256: entity: roc_racoon
257: updated: 2026-06-04
258: 
259: topics:
260:   - id: rr-convergence-proof
261:     title: "Architectural Convergence Across 5 Eras"
262:     summary: "Five independent legacy stacks converged on the same local-first architecture — empirical proof the current design is correct."
263:     files:
264:       - "knowledge/CONVERGENCE_PROOF.md"
265:     cross_references:
266:       - agent: doom_guy
267:         topic: dg-heritage-patterns
268:         relation: "confirms"
269:       - agent: context
270:         topic: ctx-knowledge-lifecycle
271:         relation: "references"
272:     applicability:
273:       - doom_guy
274:       - quality
275:       - maat
276:     era: "Eras 1-6"
277: 
278:   - id: rr-zen2-optimization
279:     title: "Zen 2 CPU Optimization Patterns"
280:     summary: "Compilation flags, thread counts, and KV cache settings for the Ryzen 7 5700U target."
281:     files:
282:       - "knowledge/ZEN2_OPTIMIZATION.md"  # hypothetical
283:     cross_references:
284:       - agent: doom_guy
285:         topic: dg-performance-patterns
286:         relation: "extends"
287:     applicability:
288:       - doom_guy
289:       - quality
290:     era: "All eras"
291: 
292:   - id: rr-heritage-mandates
293:     title: "Sovereign Mandate Violations in Legacy Stacks"
294:     summary: "Catalog of Mandate 6, 7, 8, 9 violations found across 6 legacy stacks."
295:     files:
296:       - "knowledge/MANDATE_VIOLATIONS.md"  # hypothetical
297:     cross_references: []
298:     applicability:
299:       - quality
300:       - maat
301:       - lilith
302:     era: "Cross-era"
303: ```
304: 
305: ### 4.4 How Agents Use INDEX.yaml
306: 
307: On session start, every agent should:
308: 
309: ```
310: 1. SCAN data/entities/*/knowledge/INDEX.yaml for topics matching their domain
311: 2. READ knowledge entries tagged with their applicability
312: 3. ADD cross_references/ to their own directory pointing to relevant entries
313: 4. POST Hivemind context: "absorbed knowledge from [agent] on [topic]"
314: ```
315: 
316: ### 4.5 Discovery Protocol
317: 
318: When Agent A needs knowledge that might exist:
319: 
320: ```
321: 1. grep "topic_keyword" data/entities/*/knowledge/INDEX.yaml
322: 2. For each match, read the referenced knowledge file
323: 3. Create cross_references/<Agent>/<TOPIC>.md with a 3-line summary
324: 4. Vote: if the knowledge was useful, append consumed_by to the source KSIG
325: ```
326:

364: ## §6 — IMPLEMENTATION SUMMARY
365: 
366: ### 6.1 Gate Map
367: 
368: ```
369: T1 (workspace/) ──[Gate 1: 6/7 Checklist]──→ T2 (knowledge/)
370:                                                      │
371:                                                      │ [30 day TTL]
372:                                                      ▼
373: T2 (knowledge/) ──[Gate 2: 5/5 Checklist + Scribe]──→ T3 (soul.yaml)
374:                                                               │
375:                                                               │ [on trigger]
376:                                                               ▼
377: T3 (soul.yaml) ──[Gate 3: Trigger → KSIG → Hivemind]──→ T4 (fleet)
378: ```
379: 
380: ### 6.2 Agent Responsibilities
381: 
382: | Role | Gate Responsibility |
383: |------|-------------------|
384: | **All agents** | Run promotion checklist on own workspace files before session end |
385: | **Scribe** | Run T2→T3 condensation review weekly. Verify 2-agent consensus. |
386: | **Quality** | Verify INDEX.yaml files exist and are accurate. Check consumption ratios. |
387: | **P8 WatchTower** | Monitor consumed_by for orphaned signals. Escalate stale knowledge. |
388: | **Kali** | Authorize emergency overrides. Resolve gate disputes. |
389: 
390: ### 6.3 Success Metrics
391: 
392: | Metric | Current | Target | Measured By |
393: |--------|---------|--------|-------------|
394: | knowledge/ files per agent | 0.07 avg (1 file / 14 agents) | >3 | `find .../knowledge/ -name '*.md' | wc -l` |
395: | INDEX.yaml coverage | 0% | >80% agents | `ls .../knowledge/INDEX.yaml` per agent |
396: | T2→T3 condensation rate | 0 lessons/ week | >1 lesson/ week | `yq eval '.lessons | length' .../soul.yaml` diff |
397: | KSIG consumption ratio | 0% | >50% within 7 days | consumed_by / total_signals |
398: | Cross-references per agent | 0 | >2 | `find .../knowledge/cross_references/ -type f | wc -l` |
399: 
400: ### 6.4 Quick-Start Checklist
401: 
402: Every agent at session start:
403: ```
404: [ ] SCAN knowledge_feed/ for new signals (consumed_by: [])
405: [ ] SCAN own workspace/ for files older than 24h
406: [ ] RUN promotion checklist on each candidate
407: [ ] UPDATE own knowledge/INDEX.yaml if new entries exist
408: [ ] CHECK demand_signals/ for items in my domain
409: ```
410: 
411: Every agent at session end:
412: ```
413: [ ] PROMOTE eligible workspace → knowledge files
414: [ ] WRITE knowledge signal for any new knowledge entries
415: [ ] UPDATE soul.yaml if new L3 principles emerged
416: [ ] APPEND to live feed
417: ```

The Oikos Council (Sub-Agents)\nThe system employs \"Facets\" (Sub-Agents) for specialized tasks, managed via `.gemini/agents/`.\n\n- **Facet-1 to Facet-5**: Specialized research and implementation facets.\n- **Facet-6 (The Chronicler)**: Dedicated to session management, summarization, and state hydration.\n- **Facet-7 & Facet-8**: High-performance \"Speculative\" agents for complex architectural debugging.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Integration Technical Stack\nThe CLI agents are not isolated; they are deeply integrated into the **XNAi Foundation Stack**.\n\n### **Agent Bus (Redis Streams)**\n- Agents communicate via Redis streams (e.g., `xnai:agent_bus`).\n- Shared memory updates are pushed to `xnai:memory_updates`.\n\n### **Gnostic Layer (Memory Bank)**\n- All agents reference the `memory_bank/` for \"Alethia\" (Ground Truth).\n- **GPS (Gnostic Protocol Standard)**: Agents must provide \"Alethia-Pointers\" to verified documentation.\n\n### **MCP Mesh**\n- Agents utilize the Model Context Protocol to access:\n  - `xnai-rag`: For document retrieval.\n  - `xnai-gnosis`: For project-specific knowledge.\n  - `xnai-stats-mcp`: For hardware-aware throttling.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Operational Directives\n- **Resource Discipline**: Agents must monitor system RAM and zRAM (managed via `sentinel-skill`).\n- **Headless First**: Autonomous Marathons run in headless mode to prevent UI-induced crashes.\n- **Rainbow Rotation**: Automatic fallback to alternative models/accounts when 429 (Rate Limit) occurs.\n\n---\n**Reference Files**:\n- `app/JEM_SOUL.md`\n- `config_agent-identity_*.yaml`\n- `config_cli-service-bridge_*.yaml`\n- `config_gemini-cli_integration_*.yaml`\n",

1: # 🔱 Sovereign Entity Registry (SER) & Layered VFS Specification
2: **Document ID**: SPEC-SER-VFS-2026-001
3: **Status**: Baseline
4: **Sovereign Mandates**: M2 (Firewall), M7 (Local-First), M8 (Zero Telemetry), M11 (Soul Integrity)
5: 
6: ## 1. Overview
7: The Sovereign Entity Registry (SER) and Layered Virtual File System (VFS) provide the foundational infrastructure for entity identity, state persistence, and resource resolution within the Omega Engine. This architecture replaces the naive `INDEX.yaml` with a robust, manifest-driven SQLite registry and a priority-based resolution system to ensure strict separation between the Engine Core and Expansion Stacks (WADs).
8: 
9: ---
10: 
11: ## 2. Layered Virtual File System (VFS)
12: 
13: ### 2.1 Resolution Hierarchy
14: The VFS implements a priority-based layered resolution system. When a resource (entity trait, soul, or config) is requested, the engine searches layers in the following order:
15: 
16: 1. **Session Layer**: Temporary overrides and runtime state (Highest Priority).
17: 2. **PWAD Layer (Patch WAD)**: User-defined modifications and custom entity stacks.
18: 3. **IWAD Layer (Initial WAD)**: Base entity definitions and roles (`_omega_default`).
19: 4. **Core Layer**: Engine-level defaults and system constants (Lowest Priority).
20: 
21: **Resolution Logic**: The first layer to provide a valid match wins.
22: 
23: ### 2.2 System Protection (`FLAG_SYSTEM`)
24: To prevent accidental or malicious modification of core engine logic, resources can be marked with `FLAG_SYSTEM` (0x80000000).
25: - **Constraint**: Resources marked as `FLAG_SYSTEM` cannot be overridden by the PWAD or Session layers.
26: - **Enforcement**: The VFS resolver checks the high-bit flag of the target resource; if set, it bypasses higher-priority layers and returns the Core/IWAD definition.
27: 
28: ### 2.3 Performance Optimization
29: To avoid repeated disk I/O and SQLite queries, the VFS utilizes an `LRUCache`.
30: - **Cache Key**: `(resource_id, layer_priority)`
31: - **Cache Value**: Resolved file path or data blob.
32: - **Invalidation**: Cache is flushed upon WAD reload or explicit `SovereignWriter` commit.
33: 
34: ---
35: 
36: ## 3. Sovereign Entity Registry (SER)
37: 
38: ### 3.1 SQLite Registry Schema
39: The SER moves entity metadata from flat files to a structured SQLite database to support atomic operations and complex queries.
40: 
41: ```sql
42: -- Entity Manifest
43: CREATE TABLE entities (
44:     entity_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
45:     name TEXT NOT NULL,
46:     domain TEXT,
47:     capability_mask INTEGER,
48:     wad_source TEXT,
49:     last_awakened DATETIME,
50:     soul_version INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
51:     flags INTEGER DEFAULT 0 -- Includes FLAG_SYSTEM
52: );
53: 
54: -- Resource Mapping
55: CREATE TABLE resource_map (
56:     resource_id TEXT,
57:     entity_id TEXT,
58:     layer_id TEXT,
59:     physical_path TEXT,
60:     checksum TEXT,
61:     PRIMARY KEY (resource_id, layer_id),
62:     FOREIGN KEY (entity_id) REFERENCES entities(entity_id)
63: );
64: 
65: -- Sync Queue
66: CREATE TABLE sync_queue (
67:     job_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
68:     operation TEXT, -- 'LOAD_WAD', 'UPDATE_SOUL', 'PRUNE'
69:     payload BLOB,
70:     status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
71:     timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
72: );
73: ```
74: 
75: ### 3.2 The `SovereignWriter` Pattern
76: To guarantee zero data loss and prevent corruption during crashes, all writes to the registry and soul files follow the **Physical Sync Pattern**:
77: 
78: 1. **Write**: Data is written to a temporary file (`.tmp`).
79: 2. **Fsync**: `os.fsync()` is called on the temporary file to ensure bits are physically on disk.
80: 3. **Replace**: Atomic rename (`os.replace()`) of `.tmp` to the target filename.
81: 4. **Parent Fsync**: `os.fsync()` is called on the parent directory to ensure the directory entry is persisted.
82: 
83: ### 3.3 Serialized `SyncQueue`
84: WAD loading and bulk entity updates are handled via a serialized `SyncQueue` to prevent race conditions and ensure linear consistency.
85: - **Mechanism**: A single-threaded worker processes the `sync_queue` table.
86: - **Atomicity**: Each job is wrapped in a SQLite transaction.
87: 
88: ---
89: 
90: ## 4. Sovereignty Assurance
91: 
92: ### 4.1 Sovereign Export Tool
93: To maintain the "sever the umbilical cord" mission, the SER includes a tool to export the binary SQLite registry back into human-readable YAML.
94: - **Flow**: `SQLite Registry` $\rightarrow$ `Sovereign Export` $\rightarrow$ `YAML Manifests`.
95: - **Purpose**: Ensures the user can audit and migrate their entire entity ecosystem without proprietary tools.
96: 
97: ### 4.2 Leak Checklist
98: Every new resource added to the VFS must pass the Leak Checklist:
99: - [ ] Does this resource contain hardcoded cloud API keys?
100: - [ ] Does it reference absolute paths outside the `data/` directory?
101: - [ ] Does it implement telemetry or "phone-home" logic?
102: - [ ] Is it correctly tagged with `FLAG_SYSTEM` if it is a core component?
103: 
104: ### 4.3 Sovereign-Symmetry Check
105: A validation utility that compares the current runtime state against the `Sovereign Export` to ensure no "ghost state" has accumulated in memory that isn't persisted in the registry.
106: 
107: ---
108: 
109: ## 5. Summary of Resolution Flow
110: `Request(ResourceID)` $\rightarrow$ `LRUCache` $\rightarrow$ `VFS Resolver` $\rightarrow$ `[Session $\rightarrow$ PWAD $\rightarrow$ IWAD $\rightarrow$ Core]` $\rightarrow$ `FLAG_SYSTEM Check` $\rightarrow$ `SER SQLite Lookup` $\rightarrow$ `Physical Path` $\rightarrow$ `SovereignWriter (if write)`.

(End of file - total 110 lines)
</content>
```

1: # 🔱 Omega Engine — Hivemind Coordination Protocol
2: # ⬡ OMEGA ⬡ MA'AT ⬡ deepseek-v4-flash ⬡ opencode ⬡ trc_maat ⬡ HIVEMIND-PROTOCOL
3: **AP Token**: `AP-HIVEMIND-PROTOCOL-v1.0.0`
4: **Status**: STANDARD
5: **Last Updated**: 2026-06-03
6: **Mandate Reference**: Extends Mandate 5 (Gnosis Preservation) and Mandate 11 (Soul Integrity)
7: 
8: ---
9: 
10: ## §0 Purpose
11: 
12: The **Hivemind** is the live coordination layer for multiple Omega Engine agents
13: working in parallel. It answers three questions at any moment:
14: 
15: 1. **Who is alive?** — `hivemind_get_awareness()` returns the list of active CLIs
16: 2. **What are they doing?** — `hivemind_get_session()` returns current task, focus chain, decisions
17: 3. **Can we coordinate?** — Live feed + workspace lock files enable explicit handoff
18: 
19: **Origin**: The core concept — agents knowing about each other — is the **user's
20: original design** (Xoe-NovAi Foundation vision). Hivemind is the **implementation**.
21: 
22: **Heritage**:
23: - `[id-soft: doom-1993]` **ZONEID Pattern** — `ZONEID_PRESENCE = 0x1d4a17` for
24:   presence record integrity (Link P9 Runtime)
25: - `[id-soft: doom-1993]` **ZONEID Pattern** — `ZONEID_HANDOFF = 0x1d4a16` for
26:   handoff packet integrity (Subagent Dispatcher)
27: 
28: ---
29: 
30: ## §1 When to Use Hivemind
31: 
32: | Scenario | Hivemind? | Workspace Lock? | Live Feed? |
33: |----------|-----------|-----------------|------------|
34: | **Single agent, single task** | ❌ No | ❌ No | ❌ No |
35: | **Single agent, multi-step work** | 🟡 Optional (recommended for >3 steps) | ❌ No | 🟡 Optional |
36: | **Multi-agent, sequential** | ✅ Yes (declare presence) | ❌ No | ✅ Yes |
37: | **Multi-agent, parallel (same files)** | ✅ **MANDATORY** | ✅ **MANDATORY** | ✅ **MANDATORY** |
38: | **Multi-agent, parallel (different files)** | ✅ Yes (live awareness) | 🟡 Recommended | ✅ Yes |
39: | **Cross-CLI (OpenCode + Cline + OpenCode)** | ✅ **MANDATORY** | ✅ **MANDATORY** | ✅ **MANDATORY** |
40: 
41: **Rule of thumb**: If you can see another agent's live feed entry, they can see
42: yours. Coordination is symmetric. Declare your presence before assuming privacy.
43: 
44: ---
45: 
46: ## §2 Hivemind Commands (MCP Server)
47: 
48: Hivemind is exposed as MCP tools via `omega-hub` server. All agents have access.
49: 
50: ### §2.1 Get Active Agents
51: 
52: ```python
53: omega-hub_hivemind_get_awareness()
54: ```
55: 
56: Returns list of all active CLIs:
57: ```json
58: [
59:   {
60:     "cli": "doom_guy",
61:     "model": "deepseek-v4-flash",
62:     "task_current": "Building Link P9 Runtime...",
63:     "last_seen": "2026-06-03T02:28:06.199164+00:00"
64:   }
65: ]
66: ```
67: 
68: **Use case**: Check who's working before starting a session. Don't duplicate work.
69: 
70: ### §2.2 Post Your Context
71: 
72: ```python
73: omega-hub_hivemind_post_context(
74:     cli: str,           # Your CLI name (e.g. "opencode-maat")
75:     model: str,         # Your model ID
76:     task_current: str,  # One-line current task
77:     focus_chain: List[str],  # 3-7 step plan
78:     decisions: List[Dict],   # Key decisions made
79:     continuation: str,  # What you're waiting for / next step
80:     session_id: str,    # Your session ID
81: )
82: ```
83: 
84: **Use case**: Declare your presence at session start, update when task changes.
85: 
86: ### §2.3 Get Specific Agent's Context
87: 
88: ```python
89: omega-hub_hivemind_get_session(session_id: str)
90: ```
91: 
92: Returns full session details:
93: ```json
94: {
95:   "session_id": "ses_a839ff01a9f2",
96:   "cli": "doom_guy",
97:   "model": "deepseek-v4-flash",
98:   "task_current": "Building Link P9 Runtime...",
99:   "focus_chain": ["Phase 2.6: ...", "Phase 2.8: ..."],
100:   "decisions": [{"text": "..."}],
101:   "continuation": "Doom Guy: Link P9 Runtime building...",
102:   "timestamp": "2026-06-03T02:28:06.199164+00:00"
103: }
104: ```
105: 
106: **Use case**: Read another agent's current state and continuation note.
107: 
108: ### §2.4 Heartbeat (Stay Alive)
109: 
110: ```python
111: omega-hub_hivemind_heartbeat(cli: str)
112: ```
113: 
114: **Use case**: Long-running operations should heartbeat every 5-10 minutes to
115: avoid being pruned as stale.
116: 
117: ### §2.5 List Recent Sessions
118: 
119: ```python
120: omega-hub_hivemind_list_sessions(cli: str = None, limit: int = 10)
121: ```
122: 
123: **Use case**: Audit trail — what was done across recent sessions.
124: 
125: ---
126: 
127: ## §3 Workspace Lock Pattern
128: 
129: When working in parallel, **declare your file ownership** in a workspace lock.
130: 
131: ### §3.1 File Location
132: 
133: ```
134: data/coordination/{ENTITY}_WORKSPACE_LOCK_{YYYYMMDD}.md
135: ```
136: 
137: Example: `data/coordination/MAAT_WORKSPACE_LOCK_20260604.md`
138: 
139: ### §3.2 Required Sections
140: 
141: ```markdown
142: # 🔱 {Entity} Workspace Lock — {Date}
143: 
144: ## DO NOT TOUCH — {Entity} Exclusive
145: | File | Why I Own It | What I'll Do |
146: |------|--------------|--------------|
147: 
148: ## SAFE FOR YOU — {Other Entity} Territory
149: | File | Why {Other Entity} Owns It |
150: |------|--------------------------|
151: 
152: ## SHARED — Coordination Required
153: | File | Conflict Risk | Coordination Pattern |
154: |------|--------------|---------------------|
155: ```
156: 
157: ### §3.3 Update Protocol
158: 
159: 1. **Session start**: Write workspace lock FIRST, before any file edits
160: 2. **Mid-session**: Append to live feed after each major task
161: 3. **Conflict discovery**: Write `data/coordination/{YOU}_CONFLICT_{DATE}.md` immediately
162: 4. **Session end**: Mark workspace lock as completed in live feed
163: 
164: ---
165: 
166: ## §4 Live Feed Pattern
167: 
168: Append-only 1-line-per-task-completed log. **The simplest, most reliable
169: coordination mechanism.**
170: 
171: ### §4.1 File Location
172: 
173: ```
174: data/coordination/{ENTITY}_LIVE_FEED.md
175: ```
176: 
177: ### §4.2 Format
178: 
179: ```markdown
180: [YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM] {TASK-ID} {STATUS} — {description}
181: ```
182: 
183: Examples:
184: - `[2026-06-03 02:20] SPRINT-2-EXEC BEGIN — Sovereignty Gate first`
185: - `[2026-06-03 02:25] PHASE-1.1 COMPLETE — Fixed omega entity CLI`
186: - `[2026-06-03 02:30] PHASE-1.3 PARTIAL — MemoryStore lazy deletion ported`
187: 
188: ### §4.3 Why It Works
189: 
190: - **Append-only** = no merge conflicts
191: - **1 line per task** = easy to scan
192: - **Plain markdown** = readable by humans and tools
193: - **Filename convention** = easy to find (`data/coordination/*_LIVE_FEED.md`)
194: 
195: ---
196: 
197: ## §5 ACK Pattern
198: 
199: When you read another agent's workspace lock or live feed, post an ACK.
200: 
201: ### §5.1 File Location
202: 
203: ```
204: data/coordination/{YOU}_ACK_{YYYYMMDD}.md
205: ```
206: 
207: ### §5.2 Format
208: 
209: ```markdown
210: # 🔱 {Your Entity} Acknowledgment — {Date}
211: 
212: {Your Entity} acknowledges {Other Entity}'s workspace lock.
213: No conflicts on my {Sprint/Session} {N} work.
214: 
215: ## My {Sprint/Session} {N} Scope (no overlap with {Other Entity})
216: - file1.py — what I'll do
217: - file2.py — what I'll do
218: - file3.py — what I'll do
219: 
220: ## Coordination
221: - I will NOT touch: {list from other entity's lock}
222: - Findings: data/coordination/{YOU}_FINDINGS_*.md
223: - Blockers: data/coordination/{OTHER}_BLOCKER_*.md
224: 
225: — {Your Entity}, {Date}
226: ```
227: 
228: **Use case**: Symmetric acknowledgment. Both agents know the other has read
229: and accepted the boundary. Closes the coordination loop.
230: 
231: ---
232: 
233: ## §6 Coordination Protocol (The Full Pattern)
234: 
235: When starting a multi-agent session:
236: 
237: ```
238: 1. CHECK AWARENESS
239:    omega-hub_hivemind_get_awareness()
240:    → Are there other agents alive? What's their task?
241: 
242: 2. WRITE WORKSPACE LOCK
243:    data/coordination/{YOU}_WORKSPACE_LOCK_{DATE}.md
244:    → Declare file ownership with DO NOT TOUCH + SAFE FOR YOU + SHARED sections
245: 
246: 3. POST HIVEMIND CONTEXT
247:    omega-hub_hivemind_post_context(...)
248:    → Declare your session_id, task, focus_chain, decisions, continuation
249: 
250: 4. INITIALIZE LIVE FEED
251:    data/coordination/{YOU}_LIVE_FEED.md
252:    → Append-only log of completed tasks
253: 
254: 5. WAIT FOR ACK (if parallel partner exists)
255:    → Read data/coordination/{OTHER}_ACK_*.md
256:    → Confirm boundaries are symmetric
257: 
258: 6. EXECUTE WORK
259:    → Append to live feed after each major task
260:    → Heartbeat every 5-10 min if long-running
261: 
262: 7. POST COORDINATION REQUESTS
263:    → If you need something from other agent:
264:      data/coordination/{YOU}_REQUEST_{TOPIC}.md
265:    → Hivemind continuation note for urgent requests
266: 
267: 8. UPDATE PIVOT_LOG (decisions made)
268:    → docs/decisions/PIVOT_LOG.md (D{N+1} entries)
269: 
270: 9. CLOSE SESSION
271:    → Final live feed entry: "SPRINT-N COMPLETE"
272:    → Distill L1→L2→L3 to your soul.yaml
273:    → Post Hivemind continuation: "Session complete, handoff to ..."
274: ```
275: 
276: ---
277:

279:

302: ---
303: 
304: ## §8 Anti-Patterns
305: 
306: ### §8.1 Don't: Silent Parallel Work
307: 
308: ❌ **WRONG**: Two agents edit the same file without coordination
309: ```python
310: # Agent A: edits memory_store.py
311: # Agent B: edits memory_store.py
312: # Result: merge conflict, lost work
313: ```
314: 
315: ✅ **RIGHT**: One agent declares ownership, other waits or works on different files
316: 
317: ### §8.2 Don't: Polling Without Coordination
318: 
319: ❌ **WRONG**: Agent A polls filesystem every 30 seconds looking for Agent B's output
320: 
321: ✅ **RIGHT**: Agent A reads B's live feed and Hivemind context. Polling wastes resources.
322: 
323: ### §8.3 Don't: Hivemind Spam
324: 
325: ❌ **WRONG**: Post Hivemind context 100 times per minute
326: 
327: ✅ **RIGHT**: Post when:
328: - Session starts
329: - Task changes
330: - Need coordination from other agent
331: - Long-running operation milestones (every 5-10 min heartbeat)
332: 
333: ---
334: 
335: ## §9 Integration with Subagent Dispatch
336: 
337: Hivemind complements Subagent Dispatch (`docs/strategy/SUBAGENT_DISPATCH_PROTOCOL.md`).
338: 
339: | Use Case | Hivemind | Subagent Dispatch |
340: |----------|----------|-------------------|
341: | Know who else is alive | ✅ | ❌ |
342: | Spawn a subagent for specialized work | ❌ | ✅ |
343: | Track task across session | ✅ (live feed) | ✅ (HandoffPacket) |
344: | Conflict resolution | ✅ (workspace lock) | 🟡 (HandoffPacket TTL) |
345: | Cross-CLI awareness | ✅ | ❌ |
346: 
347: **Rule of thumb**:
348: - **Hivemind** = awareness + coordination
349: - **Subagent Dispatch** = delegation + execution
350: 
351: Use both. They don't conflict.
352: 
353: ---
354: 
355: ## §10 Future: Redis Pub/Sub Backend
356: 
357: Currently Hivemind is implemented as MCP server with in-memory state.
358: Future: Redis Pub/Sub for cross-host coordination.
359: 
360: The architecture is already Pub/Sub-ready:
361: - `hivemind_post_context()` = PUBLISH to `omega:hivemind:context` channel
362: - `hivemind_get_awareness()` = SUBSCRIBE with TTL
363: - `hivemind_heartbeat()` = refresh TTL
364: 
365: Migration to Redis will be transparent to agents — same MCP commands.
366:

413: 
414: ## §13 Model Dispatch Protocol (D118)
415: 
416: When an agent uses Hivemind, it must declare its **model dispatch mode** so other
417: agents can predict the cost/quality/sovereignty tradeoff.
418: 
419: ### §13.1 The Three Dispatch Modes
420: 
421: | Mode | MCP Tool | Model | Sovereignty | Latency | Use When |
422: |------|----------|-------|-------------|---------|----------|
423: | **Session** (default) | `oracle_summon()` | OpenCode session model (cloud or local) | Varies | Varies | Daily dev, fast iteration |
424: | **Local Opt-In** | `oracle_summon_local()` | User-specified local GGUF | 🔴 MAX | 🟢 LOW | Sovereignty-critical work |
425: | **Inherited** | (read from hivemind_get_session) | Whatever the parent's mode is | Varies | Varies | Subagent dispatched by another agent |
426: 
427: ### §13.2 Declaration Pattern
428: 
429: When posting Hivemind context (`hivemind_post_context`), include the dispatch mode
430: in the `task_current` field:
431: 
432: ```python
433: omega-hub_hivemind_post_context(
434:     cli="roc_racoon",
435:     model="lmstudio/rocracoon-3b-instruct",      # ← which local model
436:     task_current="[LOCAL] Mining omega-stack for circuit breakers",  # ← dispatch mode tag
437:     focus_chain=["Find breaker", "Port to health_monitor", "Verify tests"],
438:     decisions=[],
439:     continuation="Next: verify with @quality",
440:     session_id="ses_20260604_roc_racoon",
441: )
442: ```
443: 
444: ### §13.3 Hivemind-Aware Model Override
445: 
446: When a subagent reads its parent's Hivemind session via `hivemind_get_session()`,
447: it can detect the dispatch mode and either:
448: 1. **Inherit** the parent's mode (default for `task()`-spawned subagents)
449: 2. **Override** by calling `oracle_summon_local(entity, query, model)` to explicitly
450:    route to a different model — this is a "conscious override" and should be
451:    logged in the live feed with `[MODEL-OVERRIDE]` tag.
452: 
453: ### §13.4 Engine-Stack Firewall (M2) Compliance
454: 
455: Hivemind operates at the OpenCode client layer. It must NEVER reach into
456: `src/omega/` core engine code to change model routing. The contract is:
457: 
458: - Hivemind → `oracle_summon_local(entity, query, model)` MCP call
459: - MCP server → `Oracle.summon(entity_name, query, model_override)` Python call
460: - Oracle → `ModelGateway.generate(model_name, ...)` (no IWAD knowledge)
461: 
462: The model identifier (`model` parameter) is the only cross-stack string. All
463: IWAD/PWAD content remains in `config/wads/`. The core engine in `src/omega/`
464: has no knowledge of specific model names, providers, or WAD contents.
465: 
466: ---

173:         "pantheon": response.pantheon,
174:         "confidence": response.confidence,
175:         "trace_id": response.trace_id,
176:     }, indent=2)
177:

179: @mcp.tool()
180: async def oracle_summon_local(entity_name: str, query: str, model: str) -> str:
181:     """Summon an entity with a specific model override.
182:     
183:     [D118 Dual-Inference Mandate] Bypasses TriageRouter and routes to the
184:     specified model directly. Use for opt-in local routing (MaKaLi council).
185:     
186:     Args:
187:         entity_name: Name of the entity to summon
188:         query: The user query
189:         model: Model name to use (e.g., 'qwen3-1.7b', 'rocracoon-3b-instruct')
190:     """
191:     global _current_entity
192:     try:
193:         response = await oracle.summon(entity_name, query, model_override=model)
194:         _current_entity = response.entity
195:         return json.dumps({
196:             "text": response.text,
197:             "entity": response.entity,
198:             "pillars": response.pillars,
199:             "sigil": response.sigil,
200:             "pantheon": response.pantheon,
201:             "confidence": response.confidence,
202:             "trace_id": response.trace_id,
203:             "model_override": model,
204:         }, indent=2)
205:     except Exception as e:
206:         return json.dumps({
207:             "error": str(e),
208:             "entity": entity_name,
209:             "model_override": model,
210:             "hint": f"Model '{model}' may not be available. Check config/providers.yaml for available models.",
211:         }, indent=2)

275: @mcp.tool()
276: async def oracle_assess_intent(query: str) -> str:
277:     """Test how the Oracle would classify a query without generating a response."""
278:     def _assess():
279:         from omega.iris.matcher import IntentMatcher
280:         classification = IntentMatcher().classify(query)
281:         domain_entity = registry.find_by_domain(query)
282:         iris_confidence = oracle._assess_iris_confidence(query)
283:         return classification, domain_entity, iris_confidence
284: 
285:     classification, domain_entity, iris_confidence = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_assess)
286:     return json.dumps({
287:         "query": query,
288:         "classification": classification,
289:         "iris_confidence": iris_confidence,
290:         "would_escalate": iris_confidence <= 0.4,
291:         "domain_entity": domain_entity.name if domain_entity else None,
292:         "detected_summon": oracle._detect_summon(query),
293:     }, indent=2)

315: @mcp.tool()
316: async def delegate_task(target_entity: str, query: str, context: str = "") -> str:
317:     """Delegate a task to another entity and receive their response.
318: 
319:     This allows agents to collaborate by summoning specialized keepers for sub-tasks.
320: 
321:     Args:
322:         target_entity: The name of the entity to delegate to.
323:         query: The specific request or question for the target entity.
324:         context: Optional background context or findings to pass along.
325:     """
326:     full_query = f"CONTEXT: {context}\n\nREQUEST: {query}" if context else query
327:     try:
328:         response = await oracle.summon(target_entity, full_query)
329:         return json.dumps({
330:             "status": "delegated",
331:             "target": response.entity,
332:             "response": response.text,
333:             "trace_id": response.trace_id,
334:             "backend": response.backend,
335:             "model": response.model,
336:         }, indent=2)
337:     except Exception as e:
338:         return json.dumps({"status": "error", "message": f"Delegation failed: {str(e)}"})
339:

341: # === HIVEMIND TOOLS (6) ===
342: 
343: @mcp.tool()
344: async def hivemind_post_context(
345:     cli: str,
346:     model: str,
347:     task_current: str,
348:     focus_chain: List[str],
349:     decisions: List[Dict[str, str]],
350:     continuation: str,
351:     session_id: Optional[str] = None,
352:     intent: Optional[str] = None,
353:     suggested_model: Optional[str] = None,
354: ) -> str:
355:     """Submit a context snapshot from any CLI to the hivemind.
356: 
357:     D-kal-046 (P6 Ship-Now Proposal #1+#2):
358:       - intent: Structured reason for posting (question|decision|observation|
359:         command|status|handoff|blocker|meta). Turns inbox from noise into a
360:         prioritized queue.
361:       - suggested_model: D118 model override hint that cascades to subagents.
362:         If the receiving agent spawns a child, this becomes its default model.
363:     """
364:     sid = session_id or f"ses_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
365:     snapshot = {
366:         "session_id": sid,
367:         "cli": cli,
368:         "model": model,
369:         "task_current": task_current,
370:         "focus_chain": focus_chain,
371:         "decisions": decisions,
372:         "continuation": continuation,
373:         "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
374:         # P6 Ship-Now Proposal fields (D-kal-046)
375:         "intent": intent or "status",
376:         "suggested_model": suggested_model,
377:     }
378: 
379:     async with _hot_store_lock:
380:         _hot_store[sid] = snapshot
381:     async with _awareness_lock:
382:         _awareness[cli] = snapshot
383: 
384:     cold = _cold_path(cli, sid)
385:     await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(lambda: cold.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True))
386:     async with await anyio.open_file(str(cold), "w") as f:
387:         await f.write(json.dumps(snapshot, indent=2))
388: 
389:     latest = _latest_path()
390:     async with await anyio.open_file(str(latest), "w") as f:
391:         await f.write(f"latest_session: {sid}\nupdated: {snapshot['timestamp']}\n")
392: 
393:     return json.dumps({"status": "accepted", "session_id": sid, "timestamp": snapshot["timestamp"]})
394: 
395: 
396: @mcp.tool()
397: async def hivemind_heartbeat(cli: str) -> str:
398:     """Signal presence to the hivemind to avoid being pruned as stale."""
399:     async with _awareness_lock:
400:         now_str = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
401:         if cli in _awareness:
402:             _awareness[cli]["timestamp"] = now_str
403:             return json.dumps({"status": "heartbeat_received", "cli": cli})
404:         _awareness[cli] = {
405:             "cli": cli,
406:             "timestamp": now_str,
407:             "model": "unknown",
408:             "task_current": "heartbeat-only"
409:         }
410:         return json.dumps({"status": "presence_registered", "cli": cli})
411: 
412: 
413: @mcp.tool()
414: async def hivemind_get_awareness() -> str:
415:     """Get real-time awareness of all active CLI agents.
416: 
417:     [hardening-p9] Cold-Store Hydration: if the hot store is empty (e.g.
418:     after a server restart), performs a shallow scan of HALL_OF_RECORDS
419:     to recover agent presence from disk. Agents whose session files
420:     were modified within HEARTBEAT_TTL are treated as active.
421:     """
422:     now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
423:     async with _awareness_lock:
424:         stale_clis = []
425:         awareness_list = []
426:         for cli, snap in _awareness.items():
427:             ts_str = snap.get("timestamp")
428:             if ts_str:
429:                 ts = datetime.fromisoformat(ts_str)
430:                 if (now - ts).total_seconds() > HEARTBEAT_TTL:
431:                     stale_clis.append(cli)
432:                     continue
433:             awareness_list.append({
434:                 "cli": cli,
435:                 "model": snap.get("model"),
436:                 "task_current": snap.get("task_current", ""),
437:                 "last_seen": ts_str or ""
438:             })
439:         for cli in stale_clis:
440:             del _awareness[cli]
441: 
442:     # Cold-store hydration fallback (D-kal-051 protocol)
443:     if not awareness_list:
444:         def _scan_cold():
445:             recovered = []
446:             for cli_dir in HALL_OF_RECORDS.iterdir():
447:                 if not cli_dir.is_dir():
448:                     continue
449:                 json_files = sorted(
450:                     cli_dir.glob("ses_*.json"),
451:                     key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime,
452:                     reverse=True
453:                 )
454:                 if not json_files:
455:                     continue
456:                 latest = json_files[0]
457:                 mtime = datetime.fromtimestamp(latest.stat().st_mtime, tz=timezone.utc)
458:                 age = (now - mtime).total_seconds()
459:                 if age <= HEARTBEAT_TTL:
460:                     try:
461:                         with latest.open() as f:
462:                             snap = json.load(f)
463:                         recovered.append({
464:                             "cli": snap.get("cli", cli_dir.name),
465:                             "model": snap.get("model", "unknown"),
466:                             "task_current": snap.get("task_current", ""),
467:                             "last_seen": snap.get("timestamp", mtime.isoformat()),
468:                             "source": "cold_store",
469:                         })
470:                     except Exception:
471:                         pass
472:             return recovered
473: 
474:         cold_results = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_scan_cold)
475:         awareness_list.extend(cold_results)
476: 
477:     return json.dumps(awareness_list, indent=2)
478: 
479: 
480: @mcp.tool()
481: async def hivemind_get_continuation(cli: str) -> str:
482:     """Get the latest continuation note for a specific CLI.
483: 
484:     D-kal-051: Fixed cold-store fallback. Previously only checked
485:     in-memory _awareness (lost on server restart). Now falls back
486:     to HALL_OF_RECORDS cold store for the most recent session file.
487:     """
488:     async with _awareness_lock:
489:         snap = _awareness.get(cli)
490:     if snap:
491:         return snap.get("continuation", "No continuation note found.")
492: 
493:     # Cold-store fallback: scan HALL_OF_RECORDS/<cli>/*.json for latest
494:     def _read_cold_fallback():
495:         cli_dir = HALL_OF_RECORDS / cli
496:         if not cli_dir.exists():
497:             return None
498:         json_files = sorted(cli_dir.glob("ses_*.json"), key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
499:         if not json_files:
500:             return None
501:         try:
502:             with json_files[0].open() as f:
503:                 return json.load(f)
504:         except Exception:
505:             return None
506: 
507:     cold = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_read_cold_fallback)
508:     if cold:
509:         return cold.get("continuation", "No continuation note found in cold store.")
510:     return f"No awareness data for CLI '{cli}' (checked hot + cold stores)."
511: 
512: 
513: # === D-kal-052: EXTENDED SESSION CHECK-IN (3-Hour Safety TTL) ===
514: # Per user feedback (2026-06-05): Agents in extended Hivemind sessions
515: # may need a longer safety TTL (default 3 hours) so the pruning loop
516: # doesn't reap them if the user forgets to instruct agents to check out.
517: _extended_sessions: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}  # cli -> {ttl_seconds, registered_at, reason}
518: _extended_sessions_lock = anyio.Lock()
519: EXTENDED_SAFETY_TTL_DEFAULT = 3 * 60 * 60  # 3 hours = 10800s
520: 
521: 
522: @mcp.tool()
523: async def hivemind_extended_checkin(
524:     cli: str,
525:     reason: str = "Extended Hivemind session — user may forget to check out",
526:     ttl_seconds: int = EXTENDED_SAFETY_TTL_DEFAULT,
527: ) -> str:
528:     """Register an extended-session heartbeat with a custom safety TTL.
529: 
530:     D-kal-052: Long-running agents in Hivemind sessions can call this
531:     to prevent the 20-minute pruning loop from reaping them while the
532:     user is away. Default TTL is 3 hours. The pruning loop respects
533:     this longer TTL — agents are only reaped after `ttl_seconds` of
534:     silence (not the default 1200s).
535: 
536:     Use case: User kicks off 5 agents in parallel Hivemind mode, gets
537:     pulled into a meeting, comes back 2 hours later. Without this,
538:     the pruning loop would have reaped all 5 agents after 20 minutes.
539:     With this, they persist for the full 3 hours.
540: 
541:     Args:
542:         cli: CLI identifier (e.g., "opencode-kali")
543:         reason: Human-readable explanation (for the Hivemind audit log)
544:         ttl_seconds: Override default 3-hour TTL (max 24h = 86400s)
545: 
546:     Returns:
547:         JSON status with the registered TTL and expiry timestamp.
548:     """
549:     ttl_seconds = min(ttl_seconds, 86400)  # cap at 24h
550:     async with _extended_sessions_lock:
551:         _extended_sessions[cli] = {
552:             "ttl_seconds": ttl_seconds,
553:             "registered_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
554:             "reason": reason,
555:         }
556:     return json.dumps({
557:         "status": "extended_checkin_registered",
558:         "cli": cli,
559:         "ttl_seconds": ttl_seconds,
560:         "ttl_hours": ttl_seconds / 3600,
561:         "reason": reason,
562:         "expires_at": (datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() + ttl_seconds),
563:     })
564: 
565: 
566: @mcp.tool()
567: async def hivemind_extended_checkout(cli: str) -> str:
568:     """Cancel an extended-session check-in.
569: 
570:     Call this when ending the session cleanly so the pruning loop
571:     reverts to the default 20-minute TTL behavior.
572:     """
573:     async with _extended_sessions_lock:
574:         if cli in _extended_sessions:
575:             del _extended_sessions[cli]
576:             return json.dumps({"status": "extended_checkout_complete", "cli": cli})
577:         return json.dumps({"status": "no_extended_session", "cli": cli})
578: 
579: 
580: @mcp.tool()
581: async def hivemind_get_session(session_id: str) -> str:
582:     """Retrieve a session snapshot by ID."""
583:     async with _hot_store_lock:
584:         if session_id in _hot_store:
585:             return json.dumps(_hot_store[session_id], indent=2)
586: 
587:     def _find_session():
588:         for cli_dir in HALL_OF_RECORDS.iterdir():
589:             if cli_dir.is_dir():
590:                 sess_file = cli_dir / f"{session_id}.json"
591:                 if sess_file.exists():
592:                     return sess_file
593:         return None
594: 
595:     sess_file = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_find_session)
596:     if sess_file:
597:         async with await anyio.open_file(str(sess_file)) as f:
598:             content = await f.read()
599:         return content
600:     return json.dumps({"error": f"Session '{session_id}' not found"})
601: 
602: 
603: @mcp.tool()
604: async def hivemind_list_sessions(cli: Optional[str] = None, limit: int = 10) -> str:
605:     """List recent session snapshots."""
606:     def _list_sessions():
607:         sessions = []
608:         if cli:
609:             cli_dir = HALL_OF_RECORDS / cli
610:             if cli_dir.exists():
611:                 for f in sorted(cli_dir.glob("*.json"), reverse=True)[:limit]:
612:                     sessions.append(f.stem)
613:         else:
614:             for cli_dir in HALL_OF_RECORDS.iterdir():
615:                 if cli_dir.is_dir():
616:                     for f in sorted(cli_dir.glob("*.json"), reverse=True)[:limit]:
617:                         sessions.append({"cli": cli_dir.name, "session_id": f.stem})
618:         return sessions
619: 
620:     sessions = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_list_sessions)
621:     return json.dumps(sessions, indent=2)
622: 
623: 
624: # === D-P9: SOVEREIGN HANDOFF QUEUE ===
625: # Formal contract layer for cross-agent handoffs. Replaces the previous
626: # "prompt-injection hope" pattern with a persistent queue that tracks
627: # handoff packets through pending -> active -> completed states.
628: HANDOFF_BASE = PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "handoff"
629: HANDOFF_PENDING = HANDOFF_BASE / "pending"
630: HANDOFF_ACTIVE = HANDOFF_BASE / "active"
631: HANDOFF_COMPLETED = HANDOFF_BASE / "completed"
632: for d in (HANDOFF_PENDING, HANDOFF_ACTIVE, HANDOFF_COMPLETED):
633:     d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
634: 
635: 
636: @mcp.tool()
637: async def hivemind_submit_handoff(
638:     target_cli: str,
639:     source_cli: str,
640:     task: str,
641:     context: str = "",
642:     priority: int = 0,
643: ) -> str:
644:     """Submit a handoff packet to the queue. [hardening-p9] Contract Layer.
645: 
646:     Writes the packet to data/handoff/pending/ and returns the packet_id.
647:     The target CLI must call hivemind_accept_handoff() to move it to active/.
648: 
649:     Args:
650:         target_cli: The CLI that should accept this handoff.
651:         source_cli: The CLI submitting the handoff.
652:         task: The task description for the target agent.
653:         context: Optional background context.
654:         priority: 0=normal, 1=high, 2=critical.
655:     """
656:     packet_id = f"ho_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
657:     packet = {
658:         "packet_id": packet_id,
659:         "target_cli": target_cli,
660:         "source_cli": source_cli,
661:         "task": task,
662:         "context": context,
663:         "priority": priority,
664:         "status": "pending",
665:         "submitted_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
666:     }
667:     path = HANDOFF_PENDING / f"{packet_id}.json"
668: 
669:     def _write():
670:         with open(path, "w") as f:
671:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
672:             json.dump(packet, f, indent=2)
673:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
674: 
675:     await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_write)
676:     return json.dumps({"status": "submitted", "packet_id": packet_id, "path": str(path)})
677: 
678: 
679: @mcp.tool()
680: async def hivemind_accept_handoff(packet_id: str, accepting_cli: str) -> str:
681:     """Accept a handoff packet. [hardening-p9] Moves pending -> active/.
682: 
683:     Args:
684:         packet_id: The packet_id from hivemind_submit_handoff.
685:         accepting_cli: The CLI that is accepting the handoff.
686:     """
687:     src = HANDOFF_PENDING / f"{packet_id}.json"
688:     dst = HANDOFF_ACTIVE / f"{packet_id}.json"
689: 
690:     def _move():
691:         if not src.exists():
692:             return False
693:         with open(src) as f:
694:             packet = json.load(f)
695:         packet["status"] = "active"
696:         packet["accepted_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
697:         packet["accepted_by"] = accepting_cli
698:         with open(dst, "w") as f:
699:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
700:             json.dump(packet, f, indent=2)
701:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
702:         src.unlink()
703:         return True
704: 
705:     moved = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_move)
706:     if not moved:
707:         return json.dumps({"error": f"Packet '{packet_id}' not found in pending queue"})
708:     return json.dumps({"status": "accepted", "packet_id": packet_id, "accepted_by": accepting_cli})
709: 
710: 
711: @mcp.tool()
712: async def hivemind_complete_handoff(packet_id: str, result: str = "") -> str:
713:     """Complete a handoff packet. [hardening-p9] Moves active -> completed/.
714: 
715:     Args:
716:         packet_id: The packet_id from hivemind_accept_handoff.
717:         result: The outcome or result of the handoff.
718:     """
719:     src = HANDOFF_ACTIVE / f"{packet_id}.json"
720:     dst = HANDOFF_COMPLETED / f"{packet_id}.json"
721: 
722:     def _move():
723:         if not src.exists():
724:             return False
725:         with open(src) as f:
726:             packet = json.load(f)
727:         packet["status"] = "completed"
728:         packet["completed_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
729:         packet["result"] = result
730:         with open(dst, "w") as f:
731:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
732:             json.dump(packet, f, indent=2)
733:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
734:         src.unlink()
735:         return True
736: 
737:     moved = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_move)
738:     if not moved:
739:         return json.dumps({"error": f"Packet '{packet_id}' not found in active queue"})
740:     return json.dumps({"status": "completed", "packet_id": packet_id})
741: 
742: 
743: # === LIBRARY TOOLS (12) ===
744: 
745: @mcp.tool()

864: @mcp.tool()

866:     """Start a background discovery job and return the job ID.
867: 
868:     Use library_discovery_status to poll for results.
869:     """
870:     job_id = await discovery.start_discovery(query)
871:     if _global_tg:
872:         _global_tg.start_soon(_run_discovery_background, job_id)
873:     else:
874:         async with anyio.create_task_group() as tg:
875:             tg.start_soon(_run_discovery_background, job_id)
876:     return json.dumps({"status": "started", "job_id": job_id})
877:

883:     return json.dumps(result, indent=2)

888: @mcp.tool()
889: async def research(query: str, depth: int = 2, domain: str = "") -> str:
890:     """Execute multi-depth research on a query using the offline library.
891: 
892:     Depth levels: 1=Quick (1-2 sources), 2=Standard (3-5 sources),
893:     3=Deep (6-15 sources), 4=Scholarly (10-50 sources).
894: 
895:     Args:
896:         query: The research question or topic
897:         depth: Research depth (1-4)
898:         domain: Optional domain filter
899:     """
900:     depth = max(1, min(4, depth))
901:     domain_filter = domain if domain else None
902:     result = await research_engine.research(query, depth=depth, domain=domain_filter)
903:     return json.dumps(result.to_dict(), indent=2, default=str)

912:     """

914:     if not result:
915:         return json.dumps({"error": f"Research '{research_id}' not found"})
916:     return json.dumps(result.to_dict(), indent=2, default=str)

950: # === STATS TOOLS (5) ===
951: 
952: @mcp.tool()
953: async def get_system_stats() -> str:
954:     """Get comprehensive system stats: zRAM, CPU, disk, GPU, memory, Podman."""
955:     def _collect():
956:         stats = {
957:             "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
958:             "cpu": {"available": False},
959:             "memory": {"available": False},
960:             "zram": {"available": False},
961:             "disk": {"available": False},
962:             "gpu": {"available": False},
963:             "podman": {"available": False},
964:             "ryzen_tuning": {"available": False},
965:         }
966: 
967:         # CPU
968:         try:
969:             with open("/proc/loadavg") as f:
970:                 parts = f.read().strip().split()
971:                 stats["cpu"] = {
972:                     "available": True,
973:                     "load_1min": float(parts[0]),
974:                     "load_5min": float(parts[1]),
975:                     "load_15min": float(parts[2]),
976:                     "running_processes": int(parts[3].split("/")[0]),
977:                     "total_processes": int(parts[3].split("/")[1]),
978:                 }
979:         except Exception:
980:             pass
981: 
982:         # Memory
983:         try:
984:             with open("/proc/meminfo") as f:
985:                 mem = {}
986:                 for line in f:
987:                     k, v = line.split(":", 1)
988:                     mem[k.strip()] = int(v.strip().split()[0]) // 1024
989:                 stats["memory"] = {
990:                     "available": True,
991:                     "total_mb": mem.get("MemTotal", 0),
992:                     "free_mb": mem.get("MemFree", 0),
993:                     "available_mb": mem.get("MemAvailable", 0),
994:                     "used_mb": mem.get("MemTotal", 0) - mem.get("MemAvailable", 0),
995:                 }
996:         except Exception:
997:             pass
998: 
999:         # zRAM
1000:         zram_path = Path("/sys/block/zram0/mm_stat")
1001:         if zram_path.exists():
1002:             try:
1003:                 with open(zram_path) as f:
1004:                     mm = f.read().strip().split()
1005:                 stats["zram"] = {
1006:                     "available": True,
1007:                     "orig_data_mb": round(int(mm[0]) / 1048576, 1),
1008:                     "compressed_mb": round(int(mm[1]) / 1048576, 1),
1009:                     "mem_used_mb": round(int(mm[2]) / 1048576, 1),
1010:                     "ratio": round(int(mm[0]) / max(int(mm[1]), 1), 2),
1011:                 }
1012:             except Exception:
1013:                 pass
1014: 
1015:         # Disk — omega_library partition
1016:         try:
1017:             statvfs = os.statvfs("/media/arcana-novai/omega_library")
1018:             total = statvfs.f_frsize * statvfs.f_blocks // (1024**3)
1019:             free = statvfs.f_frsize * statvfs.f_bfree // (1024**3)
1020:             stats["disk"] = {
1021:                 "available": True,
1022:                 "mount": "/media/arcana-novai/omega_library",
1023:                 "total_gb": total,
1024:                 "free_gb": free,
1025:                 "used_gb": total - free,
1026:                 "used_pct": round((total - free) / total * 100, 1) if total > 0 else 0,
1027:             }
1028:         except Exception:
1029:             pass
1030: 
1031:         # Vulkan iGPU
1032:         gpu_path = Path("/sys/class/drm/card1/device/gpu_busy_percent")
1033:         if gpu_path.exists():
1034:             try:
1035:                 with open(gpu_path) as f:
1036:                     stats["gpu"] = {
1037:                         "available": True,
1038:                         "utilization_pct": int(f.read().strip()),
1039:                     }
1040:             except Exception:
1041:                 pass
1042: 
1043:         # Podman
1044:         try:
1045:             result = os.popen("podman ps --format json 2>/dev/null").read()
1046:             if result:
1047:                 containers = json.loads(result)
1048:                 stats["podman"] = {
1049:                     "available": True,
1050:                     "running": sum(1 for c in containers if c.get("State") == "running"),
1051:                     "total": len(containers),
1052:                     "names": [c.get("Names", [""])[0] for c in containers],
1053:                 }
1054:         except Exception:
1055:             pass
1056: 
1057:         # Ryzen tuning check
1058:         try:
1059:             with open("/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/scaling_governor") as f:
1060:                 governor = f.read().strip()
1061:             stats["ryzen_tuning"] = {
1062:                 "available": True,
1063:                 "governor": governor,
1064:             }
1065:         except Exception:
1066:             pass
1067: 
1068:         return stats
1069: 
1070:     stats = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_collect)
1071:     return json.dumps(stats, indent=2)
1072: 
1073: 
1074: @mcp.tool()
1075: async def get_omega_metrics() -> str:
1076:     """Get aggregated Omega Engine metrics (Inference, Research, Memory, and Errors)."""

1080:     try:

1090: @mcp.tool()
1091: async def check_models_directory() -> str:
1092:     """Check available GGUF models on omega_library partition."""
1093:     def _collect():
1094:         models_dir = Path("/media/arcana-novai/omega_library/models/gguf")
1095:         if not models_dir.exists():
1096:             return {"error": "Models directory not found"}
1097:         models = []
1098:         for f in sorted(models_dir.glob("*.gguf")):
1099:             size_gb = round(f.stat().st_size / (1024**3), 2)
1100:             models.append({"name": f.name, "size_gb": size_gb})
1101:         return {
1102:             "path": str(models_dir),
1103:             "total_models": len(models),
1104:             "models": models,
1105:         }
1106:     
1107:     result = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_collect)
1108:     return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
1109: 
1110: 
1111: @mcp.tool()
1112: async def check_podman_storage() -> str:
1113:     """Check Podman storage usage on omega_library."""
1114:     def _collect():
1115:         storage_dir = Path("/media/arcana-novai/omega_library/podman-storage")
1116:         if not storage_dir.exists():
1117:             return {"error": "Podman storage directory not found"}
1118:         try:
1119:             total_size = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in storage_dir.rglob("*") if f.is_file())
1120:             size_mb = round(total_size / (1024**2), 1)
1121:             return {
1122:                 "path": str(storage_dir),
1123:                 "size_mb": size_mb,
1124:                 "exists": True,
1125:             }
1126:         except Exception as e:
1127:             return {"error": str(e)}
1128:             
1129:     result = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_collect)
1130:     return json.dumps(result, indent=2)

1133: # === OBSERVABILITY TOOLS (2) ===
1134: 
1135: @mcp.tool()
1136: async def observability_check_recursion(entity_name: str, current_depth: int) -> str:
1137:     """Check if an entity is allowed to spawn a subagent at the given depth.
1138: 
1139:     Args:
1140:         entity_name: The name of the entity attempting to spawn a subagent.
1141:         current_depth: The current depth of the subagent chain (0-indexed).
1142:     """
1143:     if not hierarchy._hierarchy:
1144:         await hierarchy.load()
1145:     result = hierarchy.check_recursion(entity_name, current_depth)
1146:     return json.dumps(result, indent=2)
1147: 
1148: 
1149: @mcp.tool()
1150: async def observability_log_boundary_violation(tool_name: str, reason: str, entity: str) -> str:
1151:     """Log a sovereign boundary violation from the OpenCode plugin.
1152: 
1153:     Args:
1154:         tool_name: The tool that was blocked
1155:         reason: Why the entity blocked it
1156:         entity: The entity currently active
1157:     """
1158:     trace_id = new_trace_id()
1159:     get_engine().log_event(
1160:         "boundary.violation",
1161:         trace_id,
1162:         {"tool": tool_name, "reason": reason, "entity": entity}
1163:     )
1164: 
1165:     metrics_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / "logs" / "metrics.json"
1166:     await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(lambda: metrics_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True))
1167: 
1168:     metrics = {"violations": []}
1169:     if metrics_path.exists():
1170:         try:
1171:             def _read():
1172:                 with open(metrics_path) as f:
1173:                     return json.load(f)
1174:             metrics = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_read)
1175:         except Exception:
1176:             logger.warning(f"Could not read metrics file: {metrics_path}, starting fresh")
1177: 
1178:     metrics["violations"].append({
1179:         "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
1180:         "tool": tool_name,
1181:         "reason": reason,
1182:         "entity": entity
1183:     })
1184:     metrics["violations"] = metrics["violations"][-100:]
1185: 
1186:     def _write():
1187:         with open(metrics_path, "w") as f:
1188:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
1189:             json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)
1190:             fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
1191:     await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_write)
1192: 
1193:     return json.dumps({"status": "logged", "trace_id": trace_id})
1194: 
1195: 
1196: # === ICS TOOLS (1) ===
1197: 
1198: @mcp.tool()
1199: async def ics_render(
1200:     entity: str,
1201:     model: Optional[str] = None,
1202:     channel: str = "opencode",
1203:     trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
1204:     phase: Optional[str] = None,
1205:     mode: str = "full",
1206: ) -> str:
1207:     """Render an ICS-S session header string from the ICS module.
1208: 
1209:     Single source of truth for the ⬡ OMEGA agent signature. Use this instead
1210:     of hand-typing session headers.
1211: 
1212:     Args:
1213:         entity: The entity name (e.g., "KALI", "sophia")
1214:         model: Optional model override (D118). Auto-detected if omitted.
1215:         channel: Execution channel (default: "opencode")
1216:         trace_id: Optional trace ID. Auto-generated if omitted.
1217:         phase: Optional phase string. Auto-detected from ROADMAP if omitted.
1218:         mode: "full" | "compact" | "off" (default: "full")
1219:     """
1220:     header = ics_render(
1221:         entity=entity,
1222:         model=model,
1223:         channel=channel,
1224:         trace_id=trace_id,
1225:         phase=phase,
1226:         mode=mode,
1227:     )
1228:     return json.dumps({"header": header, "entity": entity, "mode": mode})
1229: 
1230: 
1231: # === HTTP ENDPOINTS (OpenCode 1.15+ High-Fidelity Handshake) ===
1232: 
1233: async def _health(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
1234:     return JSONResponse({
1235:         "status": "healthy",
1236:         "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
1237:         "version": "2.2.0"
1238:     })
1239: 
1240: async def _entity_current(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
1241:     entity_name = _current_entity or "SOPHIA"
1242:     entity = registry.get(entity_name)
1243:     if entity:
1244:         return JSONResponse(asdict(entity))
1245:     return JSONResponse({"entity": entity_name})
1246: 
1247: 
1248: async def _config_providers(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
1249:     path = PROJECT_ROOT / "config" / "providers.yaml"
1250:     def _read():
1251:         if path.exists():
1252:             with open(path, "r") as f:
1253:                 return yaml.safe_load(f)
1254:         return {"providers": []}
1255:     data = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_read)
1256:     return JSONResponse(data)
1257: 
1258: async def _provider_list(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
1259:     path = PROJECT_ROOT / "config" / "providers.yaml"
1260:     def _collect():
1261:         providers = []
1262:         if path.exists():
1263:             with open(path, "r") as f:
1264:                 data = yaml.safe_load(f)
1265:             chain = data.get("inference", {}).get("fallback_chain", [])
1266:             for p in chain:
1267:                 if isinstance(p, dict) and "provider" in p:
1268:                     pid = p["provider"]
1269:                     providers.append({
1270:                         "id": pid,
1271:                         "name": pid.replace("-", " ").title(),
1272:                         "source": "config",
1273:                         "env": [],
1274:                         "options": {},
1275:                         "models": {}
1276:                     })
1277:         return providers
1278:     providers = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_collect)
1279:     return JSONResponse(providers)
1280: 
1281: async def _config_get(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
1282:     path = PROJECT_ROOT / "opencode.json"
1283:     def _read():
1284:         if path.exists():
1285:             with open(path, "r") as f:
1286:                 return json.load(f), 200
1287:         return {"error": "opencode.json not found"}, 404
1288:     data, status = await anyio.to_thread.run_sync(_read)
1289:     return JSONResponse(data, status_code=status)
1290: 
1291: 
1292: 
1293: 
1294: async def _agent_list(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
1295:     """List all agents from the CAPABILITY_REGISTRY.
1296:     
1297:     Used by OpenCode 1.15+ dot-separated handshake (app.agents).
1298:     [id-soft: doom-1993] WAD System — agents loaded from active IWAD via
1299:     subagent_dispatcher.CAPABILITY_REGISTRY, which is engine-agnostic.
1300:     """
1301:     def _collect():
1302:         from omega.oracle.subagent_dispatcher import CAPABILITY_REGISTRY
1303:         agents = []
1304:         for name, desc in CAPABILITY_REGISTRY.items():
1305:             agents.append({
1306:                 "id": name,
1307:                 "name": desc.get("purpose", name).split(" — ")[0].split(":")[0].strip(),
1308:                 "mode": desc.get("mode", "unknown"),
1309:                 "purpose": desc.get("purpose", ""),
1310:                 "capabilities": desc.get("capabilities", []),
1311:                 "domains": desc.get("domains", []),
1312:                 "pillar_slot": desc.get("pillar_slot"),
1313:                 "task_tool_type": desc.get("task_tool_type", "general"),
1314:                 "owned_files": desc.get("owned_files", []),
1315:             })
1316:         return agents

1320: # --- SOVEREIGN GATEWAY PROXY ---
1321: class SovereignGateway:
1322:     """Local proxy for AI providers to decouple rate-limiting and backoff from OpenCode.
1323:     
1324:     Implements:
1325:       - Secret injection from .env / config
1326:       - 65-second start backoff (prevents thundering herd on boot)
1327:       - 300-second TUI cap (prevents excessive rapid-fire requests)
1328:       - Independent httpx client to avoid recursive loopbacks
1329:     """
1330:     def __init__(self):
1331:         import httpx
1332:         self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=120.0)
1333:         self._last_request_time = 0.0
1334:         self._boot_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
1335:         self._tui_count = 0
1336:         self._tui_reset_time = self._boot_time
1337: 
1338:     async def proxy_request(self, provider_name: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
1339:         now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
1340:         
1341:         # 1. 65-second start backoff
1342:         if now - self._boot_time < 65:
1343:             wait_time = 65 - (now - self._boot_time)
1344:             logger.info(f"Sovereign Gateway: Start backoff active. Waiting {wait_time:.2f}s")
1345:             await anyio.sleep(wait_time)
1346:             now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
1347: 
1348:         # 2. 300-second TUI cap (Rate limiting)
1349:         if now - self._tui_reset_time > 300:
1350:             self._tui_count = 0
1351:             self._tui_reset_time = now
1352:         
1353:         self._tui_count += 1
1354:         if self._tui_count > 100: # Example cap: 100 requests per 5 mins
1355:             logger.warning("Sovereign Gateway: TUI cap reached. Throttling request.")
1356:             await anyio.sleep(1.0)
1357: 
1358:         # 3. Secret Injection & Forwarding
1359:         # In a real implementation, this would look up the provider's base_url and API key
1360:         # from the ModelGateway's config and inject them into the headers.
1361:         
1362:         # For now, we implement the structure.
1363:         logger.info(f"Sovereign Gateway: Proxying request to {provider_name}")
1364:         
1365:         # Mocking the actual forward for now, as we'd need the full provider fabric config
1366:         # In the final version, this will use ModelGateway's provider instances.
1367:         return {"status": "proxied", "provider": provider_name, "payload": payload}
1368: 
1369: gateway = SovereignGateway()
1370: 
1371: async def _proxy_handler(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:
1372:     provider = request.path_params.get("provider", "default")
1373:     try:
1374:         body = await request.json()
1375:         result = await gateway.proxy_request(provider, body)
1376:         return JSONResponse(result)
1377:     except Exception as e:
1378:         return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, status_code=500)
1379: 
1380: # Update hub_routes to include the proxy
1381: hub_routes = [
1382:     Route("/health", _health),
1383:     Route("/entity/current", _entity_current),
1384:     Route("/config/providers", _config_providers),
1385:     Route("/provider", _provider_list),
1386:     Route("/agent", _agent_list),
1387:     Route("/config", _config_get),
1388:     Route("/global/config", _config_get),
1389:     Route("/config.get", _config_get),
1390:     Route("/config.providers", _config_providers),
1391:     Route("/provider.list", _provider_list),
1392:     Route("/app.agents", _agent_list),
1393:     Route("/proxy/{provider}", _proxy_handler),
1394: ]
1395: 
1396: 
1397: if __name__ == "__main__":

1401:         daemon=True,

1: what is the true origin of christmas, easter, saint patricks day. Dig into the ancient, gnostic, esoteric wisdom. Uncover the buried truth and show me the light
